Conclusion: LatentGeo通过连续潜在表示学习成功解决了多模态大语言模型在辅助几何构造表示方面的挑战，提供了一种无需外部执行器或像素级渲染的有效解决方案，显著提升了几何推理能力

Abstract: Despite recent advances in multimodal reasoning, representing auxiliary geometric constructions remains a fundamental challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Such constructions are absent from the original diagram and must be introduced before theorems apply. Existing approaches predominantly rely on explicit construction paradigms, including text-based geometric specification, visual-token interleaving during reasoning, and tool-augmented geometric execution. However, these methods either fail to faithfully represent complex spatial relationships, incur representation mismatch between discrete symbols and continuous geometric structures, or rely on external capabilities that hinder end-to-end optimization. To address these limitations, we propose LatentGeo, a framework that learns continuous latent visual representations to internalize auxiliary geometric constructions without pixel-level rendering or external executors. We design a three-stage curriculum that progressively aligns and internalizes these latent representations through auxiliary visual supervision, followed by LaGDPO, a latent-aware reinforcement learning procedure that stabilizes latent representations during policy optimization while improving end-task correctness. To systematically evaluate construction-centric representation quality, we introduce GeoAux, a new benchmark targeting visually dependent geometry problems, and conduct experiments on GeoAux and MathVerse. Results show that LatentGeo achieves substantial gains on geometric reasoning tasks, particularly those requiring auxiliary constructions. Extensive analyses and ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of each component in our framework.

</details>


### [75] [BehaviorVLM: Unified Finetuning-Free Behavioral Understanding with Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12176)
*Jingyang Ke,Weihan Li,Amartya Pradhan,Jeffrey Markowitz,Anqi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BehaviorVLM：一个无需任务特定微调的统一视觉语言框架，通过引导预训练VLM进行详细推理，实现姿态估计和行为理解，大幅减少人工标注需求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前动物行为分析严重依赖人工标注或不稳定的无监督流程，限制了可扩展性和可重复性。需要开发一个既能减少人工标注又能保持准确性的统一框架

Method: 1. 姿态估计：利用量子点标记的行为数据，提出多阶段管道，整合时间、空间和跨视角推理，通过几何检查（如重投影误差）暴露低置信度标签
2. 行为理解：整合深度嵌入聚类进行过分割行为发现，基于VLM的每段视频描述，以及基于LLM的推理来合并和语义标记行为片段，可直接从视觉信息操作，无需关键点

Result: 该框架实现了可扩展、可解释且标注需求低的动物行为分析，产生的标签可用于后续过滤、校正或微调下游姿态模型

Conclusion: BehaviorVLM通过统一视觉语言框架，结合详细的推理步骤，显著减少了动物行为分析中的人工标注需求，同时提高了可扩展性和可重复性，为神经科学与自然行为的关联研究提供了实用工具

Abstract: Understanding freely moving animal behavior is central to neuroscience, where pose estimation and behavioral understanding form the foundation for linking neural activity to natural actions. Yet both tasks still depend heavily on human annotation or unstable unsupervised pipelines, limiting scalability and reproducibility. We present BehaviorVLM, a unified vision-language framework for pose estimation and behavioral understanding that requires no task-specific finetuning and minimal human labeling by guiding pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) through detailed, explicit, and verifiable reasoning steps. For pose estimation, we leverage quantum-dot-grounded behavioral data and propose a multi-stage pipeline that integrates temporal, spatial, and cross-view reasoning. This design greatly reduces human annotation effort, exposes low-confidence labels through geometric checks such as reprojection error, and produces labels that can later be filtered, corrected, or used to fine-tune downstream pose models. For behavioral understanding, we propose a pipeline that integrates deep embedded clustering for over-segmented behavior discovery, VLM-based per-clip video captioning, and LLM-based reasoning to merge and semantically label behavioral segments. The behavioral pipeline can operate directly from visual information and does not require keypoints to segment behavior. Together, these components enable scalable, interpretable, and label-light analysis of multi-animal behavior.

</details>


### [76] [ForensicZip: More Tokens are Better but Not Necessary in Forensic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12208)
*Yingxin Lai,Zitong Yu,Jun Wang,Linlin Shen,Yong Xu,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForensicZip是一个无需训练的多模态大语言模型加速框架，通过伪造驱动的视觉token剪枝方法，在保留10%token的情况下实现2.97倍加速和90%以上计算量减少，同时保持最先进的伪造检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型在多媒体取证中面临高计算成本问题，特别是处理高分辨率图像和视频时。现有的视觉token剪枝方法主要基于语义驱动，会保留显著物体但丢弃背景区域，而背景区域往往包含高频异常和时间抖动等伪造痕迹。

Method: 提出ForensicZip框架，将token压缩重新表述为伪造驱动视角。将时间token演化建模为带有松弛虚拟节点的生死最优传输问题，量化指示瞬时生成伪影的物理不连续性。取证评分进一步整合基于传输的新颖性和高频先验，在大比例压缩下分离取证证据和语义内容。

Result: 在深度伪造和AIGC基准测试中，在10%token保留率下，ForensicZip实现了2.97倍加速和超过90%的FLOPs减少，同时保持了最先进的检测性能。

Conclusion: ForensicZip通过伪造驱动的token压缩方法，有效解决了多模态大语言模型在多媒体取证中的计算效率问题，在保持检测性能的同时显著降低了计算成本，为高效多媒体取证提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) enable interpretable multimedia forensics by generating textual rationales for forgery detection. However, processing dense visual sequences incurs high computational costs, particularly for high-resolution images and videos. Visual token pruning is a practical acceleration strategy, yet existing methods are largely semantic-driven, retaining salient objects while discarding background regions where manipulation traces such as high-frequency anomalies and temporal jitters often reside. To address this issue, we introduce ForensicZip, a training-free framework that reformulates token compression from a forgery-driven perspective. ForensicZip models temporal token evolution as a Birth-Death Optimal Transport problem with a slack dummy node, quantifying physical discontinuities indicating transient generative artifacts. The forensic scoring further integrates transport-based novelty with high-frequency priors to separate forensic evidence from semantic content under large-ratio compression. Experiments on deepfake and AIGC benchmarks show that at 10\% token retention, ForensicZip achieves $2.97\times$ speedup and over 90\% FLOPs reduction while maintaining state-of-the-art detection performance.

</details>


### [77] [Real-World Point Tracking with Verifier-Guided Pseudo-Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12217)
*Görkay Aydemir,Fatma Güney,Weidi Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Verifier元模型，通过评估多个预训练跟踪器预测的可靠性来生成高质量伪标签，用于在无标注真实视频上进行高效的自训练微调


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期点跟踪模型在合成数据上训练，在真实视频中性能下降。自训练方法依赖教师模型的可靠性，但教师模型在不同帧和场景中表现不稳定，需要更可靠的伪标签生成机制

Method: 引入Verifier元模型，接收多个预训练跟踪器的候选轨迹，逐帧评估其可靠性，选择最可信的预测生成高质量伪标签轨迹，用于在无标注视频上进行数据高效的微调

Result: 在四个真实世界基准测试上取得最先进结果，同时比先前自训练方法需要更少的数据

Conclusion: Verifier引导的伪标签生成能显著提升监督质量，实现数据高效的自适应，为真实世界长期点跟踪提供有效的微调解决方案

Abstract: Models for long-term point tracking are typically trained on large synthetic datasets. The performance of these models degrades in real-world videos due to different characteristics and the absence of dense ground-truth annotations. Self-training on unlabeled videos has been explored as a practical solution, but the quality of pseudo-labels strongly depends on the reliability of teacher models, which vary across frames and scenes. In this paper, we address the problem of real-world fine-tuning and introduce verifier, a meta-model that learns to assess the reliability of tracker predictions and guide pseudo-label generation. Given candidate trajectories from multiple pretrained trackers, the verifier evaluates them per frame and selects the most trustworthy predictions, resulting in high-quality pseudo-label trajectories. When applied for fine-tuning, verifier-guided pseudo-labeling substantially improves the quality of supervision and enables data-efficient adaptation to unlabeled videos. Extensive experiments on four real-world benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art results while requiring less data than prior self-training methods. Project page: https://kuis-ai.github.io/track_on_r

</details>


### [78] [SceneAssistant: A Visual Feedback Agent for Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12238)
*Jun Luo,Jiaxiang Tang,Ruijie Lu,Gang Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneAssistant：一个基于视觉反馈驱动的智能体，通过视觉语言模型的空间推理和规划能力，结合3D对象生成模型，实现开放词汇的文本到3D场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到3D场景生成方法大多受限于特定领域或依赖预定义的空间关系，无法实现无约束的开放词汇3D场景合成。需要一种能够处理多样化自然语言描述、生成高质量3D场景的新方法。

Method: 提出SceneAssistant框架，结合现代3D对象生成模型和视觉语言模型的空间推理能力。为VLM提供一组原子操作（如缩放、旋转、聚焦），通过迭代的视觉反馈循环：VLM接收渲染的视觉反馈，采取相应操作，逐步优化场景的空间布局和文本对齐度。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够生成多样化、开放词汇的高质量3D场景。定性分析和定量人工评估均显示其优于现有方法。此外，该方法还支持用户通过自然语言指令编辑现有场景。

Conclusion: SceneAssistant通过视觉反馈驱动的智能体方法，成功实现了开放词汇的文本到3D场景生成，克服了现有方法的领域限制和空间关系约束问题，为数字内容创作提供了更灵活强大的工具。

Abstract: Text-to-3D scene generation from natural language is highly desirable for digital content creation. However, existing methods are largely domain-restricted or reliant on predefined spatial relationships, limiting their capacity for unconstrained, open-vocabulary 3D scene synthesis. In this paper, we introduce SceneAssistant, a visual-feedback-driven agent designed for open-vocabulary 3D scene generation. Our framework leverages modern 3D object generation model along with the spatial reasoning and planning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs). To enable open-vocabulary scene composition, we provide the VLMs with a comprehensive set of atomic operations (e.g., Scale, Rotate, FocusOn). At each interaction step, the VLM receives rendered visual feedback and takes actions accordingly, iteratively refining the scene to achieve more coherent spatial arrangements and better alignment with the input text. Experimental results demonstrate that our method can generate diverse, open-vocabulary, and high-quality 3D scenes. Both qualitative analysis and quantitative human evaluations demonstrate the superiority of our approach over existing methods. Furthermore, our method allows users to instruct the agent to edit existing scenes based on natural language commands. Our code is available at https://github.com/ROUJINN/SceneAssistant

</details>


### [79] [BiGain: Unified Token Compression for Joint Generation and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12240)
*Jiacheng Liu,Shengkun Tang,Jiacheng Cui,Dongkuan Xu,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BiGain是一个训练免费、即插即用的框架，通过频率分离技术同时优化加速扩散模型的生成质量和分类能力，包含拉普拉斯门控令牌合并和插值-外推KV下采样两个频率感知算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型加速方法（如令牌合并或下采样）通常只关注在减少计算量下的合成质量优化，而忽略了模型的判别能力。本文旨在重新审视令牌压缩问题，提出一个联合优化目标，在保持生成质量的同时提升加速扩散模型的分类性能。

Method: BiGain框架基于频率分离原理，将特征空间信号映射到频率感知表示中，从而分离精细细节和全局语义。包含两个核心算子：1）拉普拉斯门控令牌合并，鼓励频谱平滑令牌之间的合并，同时阻止高对比度令牌的合并，保留边缘和纹理；2）插值-外推KV下采样，通过可控的最近邻和平均池化之间的插值-外推来下采样键/值，同时保持查询完整，从而保持注意力精度。

Result: 在DiT和U-Net架构以及ImageNet-1K、ImageNet-100、Oxford-IIIT Pets和COCO-2017数据集上的实验表明，BiGain算子一致改善了扩散模型分类的速度-准确率权衡，同时在可比加速条件下保持或提升了生成质量。例如，在ImageNet-1K上，对Stable Diffusion 2.0进行70%令牌合并时，BiGain将分类准确率提高了7.15%，同时将FID改善了0.34（1.85%）。

Conclusion: 平衡的频谱保留（保留高频细节和低/中频语义）是扩散模型中令牌压缩的可靠设计原则。BiGain是第一个联合研究和推进加速扩散模型下生成和分类性能的框架，支持更低成本的部署。

Abstract: Acceleration methods for diffusion models (e.g., token merging or downsampling) typically optimize synthesis quality under reduced compute, yet often ignore discriminative capacity. We revisit token compression with a joint objective and present BiGain, a training-free, plug-and-play framework that preserves generation quality while improving classification in accelerated diffusion models. Our key insight is frequency separation: mapping feature-space signals into a frequency-aware representation disentangles fine detail from global semantics, enabling compression that respects both generative fidelity and discriminative utility. BiGain reflects this principle with two frequency-aware operators: (1) Laplacian-gated token merging, which encourages merges among spectrally smooth tokens while discouraging merges of high-contrast tokens, thereby retaining edges and textures; and (2) Interpolate-Extrapolate KV Downsampling, which downsamples keys/values via a controllable interextrapolation between nearest and average pooling while keeping queries intact, thereby conserving attention precision. Across DiT- and U-Net-based backbones and ImageNet-1K, ImageNet-100, Oxford-IIIT Pets, and COCO-2017, our operators consistently improve the speed-accuracy trade-off for diffusion-based classification, while maintaining or enhancing generation quality under comparable acceleration. For instance, on ImageNet-1K, with 70% token merging on Stable Diffusion 2.0, BiGain increases classification accuracy by 7.15% while improving FID by 0.34 (1.85%). Our analyses indicate that balanced spectral retention, preserving high-frequency detail and low/mid-frequency semantics, is a reliable design rule for token compression in diffusion models. To our knowledge, BiGain is the first framework to jointly study and advance both generation and classification under accelerated diffusion, supporting lower-cost deployment.

</details>


### [80] [One Model, Many Budgets: Elastic Latent Interfaces for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12245)
*Moayed Haji-Ali,Willi Menapace,Ivan Skorokhodov,Dogyun Park,Anil Kag,Michael Vasilkovsky,Sergey Tulyakov,Vicente Ordonez,Aliaksandr Siarohin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ELIT是一种弹性潜在接口Transformer，通过可学习的变长潜在token序列解耦图像分辨率与计算量，实现动态计算分配和重要性感知的推理调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散Transformer（DiTs）将FLOPs与图像分辨率绑定，限制了延迟-质量权衡的灵活性，且对所有空间token均匀分配计算，浪费了不重要区域的资源。

Method: 引入弹性潜在接口Transformer（ELIT），在标准Transformer块前插入潜在接口（可学习变长token序列），通过轻量级读写交叉注意力层在空间token与潜在token间传递信息，并优先处理重要区域。训练时随机丢弃尾部潜在token，使模型学习生成重要性排序的表示。

Result: 在多种数据集和架构（DiT、U-ViT、HDiT、MM-DiT）上取得一致提升。在ImageNet-1K 512px上，FID和FDD分数平均提升35.3%和39.6%。推理时可动态调整潜在token数量以适应计算约束。

Conclusion: ELIT通过解耦图像分辨率与计算量，实现了计算资源的动态分配和重要性感知的推理调整，在保持DiT兼容性的同时显著提升了生成质量和计算效率。

Abstract: Diffusion transformers (DiTs) achieve high generative quality but lock FLOPs to image resolution, limiting principled latency-quality trade-offs, and allocate computation uniformly across input spatial tokens, wasting resource allocation to unimportant regions. We introduce Elastic Latent Interface Transformer (ELIT), a drop-in, DiT-compatible mechanism that decouples input image size from compute. Our approach inserts a latent interface, a learnable variable-length token sequence on which standard transformer blocks can operate. Lightweight Read and Write cross-attention layers move information between spatial tokens and latents and prioritize important input regions. By training with random dropping of tail latents, ELIT learns to produce importance-ordered representations with earlier latents capturing global structure while later ones contain information to refine details. At inference, the number of latents can be dynamically adjusted to match compute constraints. ELIT is deliberately minimal, adding two cross-attention layers while leaving the rectified flow objective and the DiT stack unchanged. Across datasets and architectures (DiT, U-ViT, HDiT, MM-DiT), ELIT delivers consistent gains. On ImageNet-1K 512px, ELIT delivers an average gain of $35.3\%$ and $39.6\%$ in FID and FDD scores. Project page: https://snap-research.github.io/elit/

</details>


### [81] [Trust Your Critic: Robust Reward Modeling and Reinforcement Learning for Faithful Image Editing and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12247)
*Xiangyu Zhao,Peiyuan Zhang,Junming Lin,Tianhao Liang,Yuchen Duan,Shengyuan Ding,Changyao Tian,Yuhang Zang,Junchi Yan,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FIRM框架通过构建高质量评分数据集和专门奖励模型，解决RL图像编辑和生成中奖励模型的幻觉和噪声问题，提出"Base-and-Bonus"奖励策略，在多个基准上取得突破性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习中的奖励模型存在幻觉问题和噪声评分，误导图像编辑和文本到图像生成的优化过程，需要开发更准确可靠的奖励模型来指导忠实图像生成和编辑。

Method: 1) 设计专门的数据收集流程构建高质量评分数据集(FIRM-Edit-370K和FIRM-Gen-293K)；2) 训练专门奖励模型(FIRM-Edit-8B和FIRM-Gen-8B)；3) 提出"Base-and-Bonus"奖励策略：编辑使用一致性调节执行(CME)，生成使用质量调节对齐(QMA)。

Result: 1) 创建FIRM-Bench基准测试；2) 模型在人类判断对齐方面优于现有指标；3) FIRM-Qwen-Edit和FIRM-SD3.5模型实现性能突破；4) 有效缓解幻觉问题，在保真度和指令遵循方面建立新标准。

Conclusion: FIRM框架通过高质量数据集、专门奖励模型和创新的奖励策略，解决了RL图像编辑和生成中的奖励模型问题，为忠实图像生成和编辑建立了新标准，所有资源已公开。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing image editing and text-to-image (T2I) generation. However, current reward models, which act as critics during RL, often suffer from hallucinations and assign noisy scores, inherently misguiding the optimization process. In this paper, we present FIRM (Faithful Image Reward Modeling), a comprehensive framework that develops robust reward models to provide accurate and reliable guidance for faithful image generation and editing. First, we design tailored data curation pipelines to construct high-quality scoring datasets. Specifically, we evaluate editing using both execution and consistency, while generation is primarily assessed via instruction following. Using these pipelines, we collect the FIRM-Edit-370K and FIRM-Gen-293K datasets, and train specialized reward models (FIRM-Edit-8B and FIRM-Gen-8B) that accurately reflect these criteria. Second, we introduce FIRM-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark specifically designed for editing and generation critics. Evaluations demonstrate that our models achieve superior alignment with human judgment compared to existing metrics. Furthermore, to seamlessly integrate these critics into the RL pipeline, we formulate a novel "Base-and-Bonus" reward strategy that balances competing objectives: Consistency-Modulated Execution (CME) for editing and Quality-Modulated Alignment (QMA) for generation. Empowered by this framework, our resulting models FIRM-Qwen-Edit and FIRM-SD3.5 achieve substantial performance breakthroughs. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that FIRM mitigates hallucinations, establishing a new standard for fidelity and instruction adherence over existing general models. All of our datasets, models, and code have been publicly available at https://firm-reward.github.io.

</details>


### [82] [DVD: Deterministic Video Depth Estimation with Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12250)
*Hongfei Zhang,Harold Haodong Chen,Chenfei Liao,Jing He,Zixin Zhang,Haodong Li,Yihao Liang,Kanghao Chen,Bin Ren,Xu Zheng,Shuai Yang,Kun Zhou,Yinchuan Li,Nicu Sebe,Ying-Cong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DVD框架将预训练视频扩散模型转化为单次深度回归器，解决视频深度估计中生成模型与判别模型的权衡问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频深度估计面临基本权衡：生成模型存在随机几何幻觉和尺度漂移，而判别模型需要大量标注数据来解决语义歧义。需要打破这种僵局。

Method: DVD框架包含三个核心设计：(1) 将扩散时间步重新用作结构锚点，平衡全局稳定性与高频细节；(2) 潜在流形校正(LMR)缓解回归引起的过度平滑，通过微分约束恢复锐利边界和连贯运动；(3) 全局仿射相干性，固有属性限制窗口间发散，实现无需复杂时间对齐的长视频推理。

Result: DVD在多个基准测试中实现了最先进的零样本性能，仅使用领先基线1/163的任务特定数据就成功解锁了视频基础模型中隐含的深度几何先验。

Conclusion: DVD是首个将预训练视频扩散模型确定性适应为单次深度回归器的框架，解决了视频深度估计的核心挑战，并开源了整个训练套件以促进开源社区发展。

Abstract: Existing video depth estimation faces a fundamental trade-off: generative models suffer from stochastic geometric hallucinations and scale drift, while discriminative models demand massive labeled datasets to resolve semantic ambiguities. To break this impasse, we present DVD, the first framework to deterministically adapt pre-trained video diffusion models into single-pass depth regressors. Specifically, DVD features three core designs: (i) repurposing the diffusion timestep as a structural anchor to balance global stability with high-frequency details; (ii) latent manifold rectification (LMR) to mitigate regression-induced over-smoothing, enforcing differential constraints to restore sharp boundaries and coherent motion; and (iii) global affine coherence, an inherent property bounding inter-window divergence, which enables seamless long-video inference without requiring complex temporal alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DVD achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance across benchmarks. Furthermore, DVD successfully unlocks the profound geometric priors implicit in video foundation models using 163x less task-specific data than leading baselines. Notably, we fully release our pipeline, providing the whole training suite for SOTA video depth estimation to benefit the open-source community.

</details>


### [83] [EndoCoT: Scaling Endogenous Chain-of-Thought Reasoning in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12252)
*Xuanlang Dai,Yujie Zhou,Long Xing,Jiazi Bu,Xilin Wei,Yuhong Liu,Beichen Zhang,Kai Chen,Yuhang Zang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EndoCoT框架通过迭代思维引导模块激活MLLMs的推理潜力，并将思维状态连接到DiT的去噪过程，解决MLLMs在扩散模型中作为文本编码器时推理深度不足和引导不变的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前将多模态大语言模型集成到扩散框架作为文本编码器的范式存在两个关键限制：1) MLLMs文本编码器推理深度不足，单步编码无法激活思维链过程；2) 解码过程中的引导保持不变，即使有正确的MLLM编码，DiT也无法逐步分解复杂指令为可执行的去噪步骤。

Method: 提出Endogenous Chain-of-Thought框架：1) 通过迭代思维引导模块迭代细化潜在思维状态，激活MLLMs的推理潜力，并将这些状态连接到DiT的去噪过程；2) 应用终端思维接地模块，通过将最终状态与真实答案对齐，确保推理轨迹保持基于文本监督。

Result: 在多个基准测试（Maze、TSP、VSP和Sudoku）上的广泛评估实现了92.1%的平均准确率，比最强基线高出8.3个百分点。

Conclusion: EndoCoT框架使MLLM文本编码器能够提供精心推理的引导，使DiT能够逐步执行并最终以分步方式解决复杂任务，显著提升了扩散模型在复杂空间推理任务上的性能。

Abstract: Recently, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have been widely integrated into diffusion frameworks primarily as text encoders to tackle complex tasks such as spatial reasoning. However, this paradigm suffers from two critical limitations: (i) MLLMs text encoder exhibits insufficient reasoning depth. Single-step encoding fails to activate the Chain-of-Thought process, which is essential for MLLMs to provide accurate guidance for complex tasks. (ii) The guidance remains invariant during the decoding process. Invariant guidance during decoding prevents DiT from progressively decomposing complex instructions into actionable denoising steps, even with correct MLLM encodings. To this end, we propose Endogenous Chain-of-Thought (EndoCoT), a novel framework that first activates MLLMs' reasoning potential by iteratively refining latent thought states through an iterative thought guidance module, and then bridges these states to the DiT's denoising process. Second, a terminal thought grounding module is applied to ensure the reasoning trajectory remains grounded in textual supervision by aligning the final state with ground-truth answers. With these two components, the MLLM text encoder delivers meticulously reasoned guidance, enabling the DiT to execute it progressively and ultimately solve complex tasks in a step-by-step manner. Extensive evaluations across diverse benchmarks (e.g., Maze, TSP, VSP, and Sudoku) achieve an average accuracy of 92.1%, outperforming the strongest baseline by 8.3 percentage points.

</details>


### [84] [Attend Before Attention: Efficient and Scalable Video Understanding via Autoregressive Gazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12254)
*Baifeng Shi,Stephanie Fu,Long Lian,Hanrong Ye,David Eigen,Aaron Reite,Boyi Li,Jan Kautz,Song Han,David M. Chan,Pavlo Molchanov,Trevor Darrell,Hongxu Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoGaze通过自回归选择最小多尺度补丁集来减少视频处理中的时空冗余，显著降低视觉token数量并加速ViT和MLLM处理，使其能够扩展到1K帧4K分辨率视频，在视频基准测试中取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在处理长时高分辨率视频时面临挑战，因为它们在视觉变换器或LLM中对每个像素进行同等处理，忽略了显著的时空冗余，导致计算效率低下。

Method: AutoGaze是一个轻量级模块，通过下一token预测和强化学习训练，自回归地选择能够以用户指定误差阈值重建视频的最小多尺度补丁集，在ViT或MLLM处理前去除冗余补丁。

Result: AutoGaze将视觉token减少4-100倍，加速ViT和MLLM处理达19倍，使MLLM能够扩展到1K帧4K分辨率视频，在VideoMME上达到67.0%的优异表现。在HLVid基准测试中，使用AutoGaze的MLLM比基线提升10.1%，比先前最佳MLLM提升4.5%。

Conclusion: AutoGaze通过有效去除视频中的时空冗余，显著提升了多模态大语言模型处理长时高分辨率视频的效率和性能，为视频理解任务提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced general-purpose video understanding but struggle with long, high-resolution videos -- they process every pixel equally in their vision transformers (ViTs) or LLMs despite significant spatiotemporal redundancy. We introduce AutoGaze, a lightweight module that removes redundant patches before processed by a ViT or an MLLM. Trained with next-token prediction and reinforcement learning, AutoGaze autoregressively selects a minimal set of multi-scale patches that can reconstruct the video within a user-specified error threshold, eliminating redundancy while preserving information. Empirically, AutoGaze reduces visual tokens by 4x-100x and accelerates ViTs and MLLMs by up to 19x, enabling scaling MLLMs to 1K-frame 4K-resolution videos and achieving superior results on video benchmarks (e.g., 67.0% on VideoMME). Furthermore, we introduce HLVid: the first high-resolution, long-form video QA benchmark with 5-minute 4K-resolution videos, where an MLLM scaled with AutoGaze improves over the baseline by 10.1% and outperforms the previous best MLLM by 4.5%. Project page: https://autogaze.github.io/.

</details>


### [85] [Spatial-TTT: Streaming Visual-based Spatial Intelligence with Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12255)
*Fangfu Liu,Diankun Wu,Jiawei Chi,Yimo Cai,Yi-Hsin Hung,Xumin Yu,Hao Li,Han Hu,Yongming Rao,Yueqi Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spatial-TTT：一种基于测试时训练（TTT）的流式视觉空间智能方法，通过自适应更新快速权重来从长时视频中捕获和组织空间证据


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过连续的视觉观察来感知和理解真实世界空间，因此需要能够从潜在无限长的视频流中持续维护和更新空间证据的能力。核心挑战不仅在于更长的上下文窗口，更在于如何选择、组织和长期保留空间信息。

Method: 提出Spatial-TTT方法，采用测试时训练（TTT）自适应更新参数子集（快速权重）来捕获和组织长时场景视频中的空间证据。设计混合架构，采用大块更新与滑动窗口注意力并行处理空间视频。引入空间预测机制，应用于TTT层的3D时空卷积，以促进几何对应性和时间连续性的捕捉。构建具有密集3D空间描述的数据集，指导模型更新快速权重以结构化方式记忆和组织全局3D空间信号。

Result: 大量实验表明，Spatial-TTT提升了长时空间理解能力，在视频空间基准测试中达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: Spatial-TTT通过测试时训练和专门设计的架构，有效解决了从长时视频流中持续维护和更新空间证据的挑战，为流式视觉空间智能提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Humans perceive and understand real-world spaces through a stream of visual observations. Therefore, the ability to streamingly maintain and update spatial evidence from potentially unbounded video streams is essential for spatial intelligence. The core challenge is not simply longer context windows but how spatial information is selected, organized, and retained over time. In this paper, we propose Spatial-TTT towards streaming visual-based spatial intelligence with test-time training (TTT), which adapts a subset of parameters (fast weights) to capture and organize spatial evidence over long-horizon scene videos. Specifically, we design a hybrid architecture and adopt large-chunk updates parallel with sliding-window attention for efficient spatial video processing. To further promote spatial awareness, we introduce a spatial-predictive mechanism applied to TTT layers with 3D spatiotemporal convolution, which encourages the model to capture geometric correspondence and temporal continuity across frames. Beyond architecture design, we construct a dataset with dense 3D spatial descriptions, which guides the model to update its fast weights to memorize and organize global 3D spatial signals in a structured manner. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Spatial-TTT improves long-horizon spatial understanding and achieves state-of-the-art performance on video spatial benchmarks. Project page: https://liuff19.github.io/Spatial-TTT.

</details>


### [86] [DreamVideo-Omni: Omni-Motion Controlled Multi-Subject Video Customization with Latent Identity Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12257)
*Yujie Wei,Xinyu Liu,Shiwei Zhang,Hangjie Yuan,Jinbo Xing,Zhekai Chen,Xiang Wang,Haonan Qiu,Rui Zhao,Yutong Feng,Ruihang Chu,Yingya Zhang,Yike Guo,Xihui Liu,Hongming Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamVideo-Omni：一个统一框架，通过渐进式两阶段训练范式实现多主体定制与全方位运动控制，解决现有方法在运动粒度、控制模糊性和身份退化方面的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模扩散模型虽然革新了视频合成，但在多主体身份和多粒度运动的精确控制方面仍面临重大挑战。现有方法存在运动粒度有限、控制模糊和身份退化等问题，导致身份保持和运动控制性能不佳。

Method: 采用渐进式两阶段训练范式：第一阶段整合主体外观、全局运动、局部动态和相机运动等全面控制信号进行联合训练，引入条件感知3D旋转位置嵌入协调异构输入，以及分层运动注入策略增强全局运动引导；引入组和角色嵌入明确将运动信号锚定到特定身份，解耦复杂场景为独立可控实例。第二阶段设计潜在身份奖励反馈学习范式，在预训练视频扩散骨干上训练潜在身份奖励模型，在潜在空间提供运动感知的身份奖励，优先保持符合人类偏好的身份。

Result: 基于构建的大规模数据集和全面的DreamOmni Bench评估基准，DreamVideo-Omni在生成高质量视频和精确可控性方面表现出优越性能。

Conclusion: DreamVideo-Omni通过创新的两阶段训练架构和专门设计的控制机制，成功解决了多主体视频合成中的身份保持和全方位运动控制难题，为视频生成领域提供了更精确可控的解决方案。

Abstract: While large-scale diffusion models have revolutionized video synthesis, achieving precise control over both multi-subject identity and multi-granularity motion remains a significant challenge. Recent attempts to bridge this gap often suffer from limited motion granularity, control ambiguity, and identity degradation, leading to suboptimal performance on identity preservation and motion control. In this work, we present DreamVideo-Omni, a unified framework enabling harmonious multi-subject customization with omni-motion control via a progressive two-stage training paradigm. In the first stage, we integrate comprehensive control signals for joint training, encompassing subject appearances, global motion, local dynamics, and camera movements. To ensure robust and precise controllability, we introduce a condition-aware 3D rotary positional embedding to coordinate heterogeneous inputs and a hierarchical motion injection strategy to enhance global motion guidance. Furthermore, to resolve multi-subject ambiguity, we introduce group and role embeddings to explicitly anchor motion signals to specific identities, effectively disentangling complex scenes into independent controllable instances. In the second stage, to mitigate identity degradation, we design a latent identity reward feedback learning paradigm by training a latent identity reward model upon a pretrained video diffusion backbone. This provides motion-aware identity rewards in the latent space, prioritizing identity preservation aligned with human preferences. Supported by our curated large-scale dataset and the comprehensive DreamOmni Bench for multi-subject and omni-motion control evaluation, DreamVideo-Omni demonstrates superior performance in generating high-quality videos with precise controllability.

</details>


### [87] [Video Streaming Thinking: VideoLLMs Can Watch and Think Simultaneously](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12262)
*Yiran Guan,Liang Yin,Dingkang Liang,Jianzhong Ju,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan,Yuliang Liu,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Video Streaming Thinking (VST) 是一种支持"边看边思考"机制的新型流式视频理解范式，通过在视频播放过程中分摊LLM推理延迟，在保持实时响应的同时提升理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式感知方法缺乏同步的逻辑推理流，而直接应用测试时缩放方法会导致不可接受的响应延迟，需要在实时响应和深度理解之间找到平衡。

Method: 提出VST范式，支持"边看边思考"机制，在流式视频片段到达时激活推理。包含完整的后训练流程：VST-SFT结构化适配离线VideoLLM到因果流式推理，VST-RL通过多轮视频交互环境中的自我探索进行端到端改进。还设计了自动化训练数据合成流程，使用视频知识图谱生成高质量流式QA对，并采用实体关系基础的流式思维链来强制多证据推理和持续关注视频流。

Result: VST-7B在在线基准测试中表现优异：StreamingBench上79.5%，OVO-Bench上59.3%。相比Video-R1，VST响应速度快15.7倍，在VideoHolmes上提升+5.4%，在保持离线长形式或推理基准竞争力的同时，展示了更高的效率和跨多样化视频理解任务的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: VST通过创新的"边看边思考"机制有效解决了流式视频理解中实时响应与深度推理的权衡问题，提供了一种高效且通用的视频流理解解决方案。

Abstract: Online Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) play a critical role in supporting responsive, real-time interaction. Existing methods focus on streaming perception, lacking a synchronized logical reasoning stream. However, directly applying test-time scaling methods incurs unacceptable response latency. To address this trade-off, we propose Video Streaming Thinking (VST), a novel paradigm for streaming video understanding. It supports a thinking while watching mechanism, which activates reasoning over incoming video clips during streaming. This design improves timely comprehension and coherent cognition while preserving real-time responsiveness by amortizing LLM reasoning latency over video playback. Furthermore, we introduce a comprehensive post-training pipeline that integrates VST-SFT, which structurally adapts the offline VideoLLM to causal streaming reasoning, and VST-RL, which provides end-to-end improvement through self-exploration in a multi-turn video interaction environment. Additionally, we devise an automated training-data synthesis pipeline that uses video knowledge graphs to generate high-quality streaming QA pairs, with an entity-relation grounded streaming Chain-of-Thought to enforce multi-evidence reasoning and sustained attention to the video stream. Extensive evaluations show that VST-7B performs strongly on online benchmarks, e.g. 79.5% on StreamingBench and 59.3% on OVO-Bench. Meanwhile, VST remains competitive on offline long-form or reasoning benchmarks. Compared with Video-R1, VST responds 15.7 times faster and achieves +5.4% improvement on VideoHolmes, demonstrating higher efficiency and strong generalization across diverse video understanding tasks. Code, data, and models will be released at https://github.com/1ranGuan/VST.

</details>


### [88] [GRADE: Benchmarking Discipline-Informed Reasoning in Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12264)
*Mingxin Liu,Ziqian Fan,Zhaokai Wang,Leyao Gu,Zirun Zhu,Yiguo He,Yuchen Yang,Changyao Tian,Xiangyu Zhao,Ning Liao,Shaofeng Zhang,Qibing Ren,Zhihang Zhong,Xuanhe Zhou,Junchi Yan,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRADE是首个评估学科知识推理能力的图像编辑基准，包含10个学术领域的520个样本，采用多维度评估协议，揭示了当前模型在知识密集型编辑任务中的显著局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前统一多模态模型旨在实现联合理解、推理和生成，但现有图像编辑基准主要局限于自然图像和浅层常识推理，缺乏在结构化、领域特定约束下评估这些能力的方法。

Method: 提出GRADE基准，包含10个学术领域（从自然科学到社会科学）的520个精心策划样本；设计多维度评估协议，联合评估学科推理、视觉一致性和逻辑可读性；对20个最先进的开源和闭源模型进行广泛实验。

Result: 实验显示当前模型在隐含、知识密集型编辑场景中存在显著局限性，导致较大性能差距；通过严格分析和消融实验揭示了模型缺陷和学科编辑中的约束条件。

Conclusion: GRADE为统一多模态模型的未来发展指明了关键方向，推动了学科知识驱动的图像编辑和推理研究；基准和评估代码已公开发布。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models target joint understanding, reasoning, and generation, but current image editing benchmarks are largely confined to natural images and shallow commonsense reasoning, offering limited assessment of this capability under structured, domain-specific constraints. In this work, we introduce GRADE, the first benchmark to assess discipline-informed knowledge and reasoning in image editing. GRADE comprises 520 carefully curated samples across 10 academic domains, spanning from natural science to social science. To support rigorous evaluation, we propose a multi-dimensional evaluation protocol that jointly assesses Discipline Reasoning, Visual Consistency, and Logical Readability. Extensive experiments on 20 state-of-the-art open-source and closed-source models reveal substantial limitations in current models under implicit, knowledge-intensive editing settings, leading to large performance gaps. Beyond quantitative scores, we conduct rigorous analyses and ablations to expose model shortcomings and identify the constraints within disciplinary editing. Together, GRADE pinpoints key directions for the future development of unified multimodal models, advancing the research on discipline-informed image editing and reasoning. Our benchmark and evaluation code are publicly released.

</details>


### [89] [EVATok: Adaptive Length Video Tokenization for Efficient Visual Autoregressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12267)
*Tianwei Xiong,Jun Hao Liew,Zilong Huang,Zhijie Lin,Jiashi Feng,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EVATok是一个高效视频自适应分词器框架，通过为每个视频估计最优分词分配来平衡重建质量和计算成本，相比传统均匀分词方法节省至少24.4%的平均分词使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频分词器在不同视频的时间块上采用均匀分词分配，导致在简单、静态或重复片段上浪费分词，而在动态或复杂片段上分词不足，造成效率低下和质量损失。

Method: 提出EVATok框架：1) 为每个视频估计最优分词分配以实现最佳质量-成本权衡；2) 开发轻量级路由器快速预测这些最优分配；3) 训练自适应分词器根据路由器预测的分配编码视频；4) 集成视频语义编码器的高级训练配方。

Result: EVATok在视频重建和下游自回归生成方面显著提升了效率和质量。在UCF-101数据集上实现了卓越的重建效果和最先进的类别到视频生成性能，相比先前最先进的LARP和固定长度基线至少节省24.4%的平均分词使用量。

Conclusion: EVATok通过自适应分词分配有效解决了传统均匀分词方法的效率问题，在保持高质量重建的同时显著降低了计算成本，为视频生成任务提供了更高效的分词解决方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video generative models rely on video tokenizers that compress pixels into discrete token sequences. The length of these token sequences is crucial for balancing reconstruction quality against downstream generation computational cost. Traditional video tokenizers apply a uniform token assignment across temporal blocks of different videos, often wasting tokens on simple, static, or repetitive segments while underserving dynamic or complex ones. To address this inefficiency, we introduce $\textbf{EVATok}$, a framework to produce $\textbf{E}$fficient $\textbf{V}$ideo $\textbf{A}$daptive $\textbf{Tok}$enizers. Our framework estimates optimal token assignments for each video to achieve the best quality-cost trade-off, develops lightweight routers for fast prediction of these optimal assignments, and trains adaptive tokenizers that encode videos based on the assignments predicted by routers. We demonstrate that EVATok delivers substantial improvements in efficiency and overall quality for video reconstruction and downstream AR generation. Enhanced by our advanced training recipe that integrates video semantic encoders, EVATok achieves superior reconstruction and state-of-the-art class-to-video generation on UCF-101, with at least 24.4% savings in average token usage compared to the prior state-of-the-art LARP and our fixed-length baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [OA-NBV: Occlusion-Aware Next-Best-View Planning for Human-Centered Active Perception on Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11072)
*Boxun Hu,Chang Chang,Jiawei Ge,Man Namgung,Xiaomin Lin,Axel Krieger,Tinoosh Mohsenin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OA-NBV是一种用于移动机器人的遮挡感知最佳视点规划方法，专门针对被遮挡人体的观测优化，通过目标中心可见性模型评分候选视点，在仿真和真实实验中达到90%以上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类中心操作（如搜索、分类、灾难响应）中，杂乱环境和部分可见性经常降低下游感知性能。现有最佳视点规划方法主要优化通用探索或长时程覆盖，未针对在真实运动约束下获取被遮挡人员的单个可用观测这一即时目标。

Method: OA-NBV集成了感知和运动规划，使用目标中心可见性模型对候选视点进行评分，该模型考虑了遮挡、目标尺度和目标完整性，同时将候选视点限制在可行的机器人姿态。该方法作为即插即用的视点选择模块，适用于多样化的人类中心下游任务。

Result: OA-NBV在仿真和真实世界试验中均达到超过90%的成功率，而基线NBV方法在遮挡情况下性能急剧下降。在观测质量方面，相比最强基线，OA-NBV将归一化目标面积至少提高81%，关键点可见性至少提高58%。

Conclusion: OA-NBV是一种有效的遮挡感知最佳视点规划方法，能够自主选择可遍历视点以获得被遮挡人类更完整的视图，显著提高了人类中心主动感知的成功率和观测质量。

Abstract: We naturally step sideways or lean to see around the obstacle when our view is blocked, and recover a more informative observation. Enabling robots to make the same kind of viewpoint choice is critical for human-centered operations, including search, triage, and disaster response, where cluttered environments and partial visibility frequently degrade downstream perception. However, many Next-Best-View (NBV) methods primarily optimize generic exploration or long-horizon coverage, and do not explicitly target the immediate goal of obtaining a single usable observation of a partially occluded person under real motion constraints. We present Occlusion-Aware Next-Best-View Planning for Human-Centered Active Perception on Mobile Robots (OA-NBV), an occlusion-aware NBV pipeline that autonomously selects the next traversable viewpoint to obtain a more complete view of an occluded human. OA-NBV integrates perception and motion planning by scoring candidate viewpoints using a target-centric visibility model that accounts for occlusion, target scale, and target completeness, while restricting candidates to feasible robot poses. OA-NBV achieves over 90% success rate in both simulation and real-world trials, while baseline NBV methods degrade sharply under occlusion. Beyond success rate, OA-NBV improves observation quality: compared to the strongest baseline, it increases normalized target area by at least 81% and keypoint visibility by at least 58% across settings, making it a drop-in view-selection module for diverse human-centered downstream tasks.

</details>


### [91] [Edge-Assisted Multi-Robot Visual-Inertial SLAM with Efficient Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11085)
*Xin Liu,Shuhuan Wen,Jing Zhao,Tony Z. Qiu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于机器人-边缘-云分层架构的集中式多机器人SLAM系统，采用基于金字塔IMU预测的光流跟踪轻量化SLAM方法，通过特征点和关键帧描述符的无损编码压缩实现有限带宽下的实时远程传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云边计算融合是实现全局一致实时多机器人SLAM的有效途径，但终端设备与云之间的有限带宽和长通信链路导致系统性能严重下降，同时单机器人存在机载计算资源有限和执行效率低的问题。

Method: 1) 提出基于金字塔IMU预测的光流跟踪轻量化SLAM方法，降低特征跟踪计算成本；2) 设计机器人-边缘-云分层架构的集中式多机器人SLAM系统；3) 仅传输特征点和关键帧描述符，并进行无损编码压缩，实现有限带宽下的实时远程传输。

Result: 在EuRoC数据集上的实验验证表明：1) 相比当前最先进的局部特征压缩方法，能实现更低数据量的特征传输；2) 相比当前先进的集中式多机器人SLAM方案，在低计算负载下能达到相同或更好的定位精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层架构和轻量化SLAM方法有效解决了多机器人SLAM系统中的带宽限制和计算资源约束问题，在保证定位精度的同时实现了实时协同SLAM。

Abstract: The integration of cloud computing and edge computing is an effective way to achieve global consistent and real-time multi-robot Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM). Cloud computing effectively solves the problem of limited computing, communication and storage capacity of terminal equipment. However, limited bandwidth and extremely long communication links between terminal devices and the cloud result in serious performance degradation of multi-robot SLAM systems. To reduce the computational cost of feature tracking and improve the real-time performance of the robot, a lightweight SLAM method of optical flow tracking based on pyramid IMU prediction is proposed. On this basis, a centralized multi-robot SLAM system based on a robot-edge-cloud layered architecture is proposed to realize real-time collaborative SLAM. It avoids the problems of limited on-board computing resources and low execution efficiency of single robot. In this framework, only the feature points and keyframe descriptors are transmitted and lossless encoding and compression are carried out to realize real-time remote information transmission with limited bandwidth resources. This design reduces the actual bandwidth occupied in the process of data transmission, and does not cause the loss of SLAM accuracy caused by data compression. Through experimental verification on the EuRoC dataset, compared with the current most advanced local feature compression method, our method can achieve lower data volume feature transmission, and compared with the current advanced centralized multi-robot SLAM scheme, it can achieve the same or better positioning accuracy under low computational load.

</details>


### [92] [ResWM: Residual-Action World Model for Visual RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11110)
*Jseen Zhang,Gabriel Adineera,Jinzhou Tan,Jinoh Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ResWM：一种将控制变量从绝对动作重构为残差动作（相对于上一步的增量调整）的世界模型框架，通过观测差异编码器建模帧间变化，在Dreamer风格潜在动力学模型中实现更平滑探索和稳定规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型的RL框架直接基于绝对动作进行未来预测，导致优化不稳定：最优动作分布是任务依赖的、先验未知的，常导致振荡或低效控制。需要一种更符合真实世界控制平滑特性的框架。

Method: 提出残差动作世界模型（ResWM），将控制变量从绝对动作重构为残差动作（增量调整）。引入观测差异编码器显式建模相邻帧间变化，生成紧凑的潜在动力学表示。在Dreamer风格潜在动力学模型中集成ResWM，无需额外超参数，在残差动作空间进行想象展开和策略优化。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite上的实验表明，ResWM在样本效率、渐进回报和控制平滑性方面实现一致改进，显著超越Dreamer和TD-MPC等强基线。产生更稳定、能量效率更高的动作轨迹，这对真实世界机器人系统部署至关重要。

Conclusion: 残差动作建模为连接RL算法进展与机器人实际需求提供了简单而强大的原则，通过将控制变量重构为增量调整，实现了更平滑探索、更低控制方差和更可靠规划。

Abstract: Learning predictive world models from raw visual observations is a central challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), especially for robotics and continuous control. Conventional model-based RL frameworks directly condition future predictions on absolute actions, which makes optimization unstable: the optimal action distributions are task-dependent, unknown a priori, and often lead to oscillatory or inefficient control. To address this, we introduce the Residual-Action World Model (ResWM), a new framework that reformulates the control variable from absolute actions to residual actions -- incremental adjustments relative to the previous step. This design aligns with the inherent smoothness of real-world control, reduces the effective search space, and stabilizes long-horizon planning. To further strengthen the representation, we propose an Observation Difference Encoder that explicitly models the changes between adjacent frames, yielding compact latent dynamics that are naturally coupled with residual actions. ResWM is integrated into a Dreamer-style latent dynamics model with minimal modifications and no extra hyperparameters. Both imagination rollouts and policy optimization are conducted in the residual-action space, enabling smoother exploration, lower control variance, and more reliable planning. Empirical results on the DeepMind Control Suite demonstrate that ResWM achieves consistent improvements in sample efficiency, asymptotic returns, and control smoothness, significantly surpassing strong baselines such as Dreamer and TD-MPC. Beyond performance, ResWM produces more stable and energy-efficient action trajectories, a property critical for robotic systems deployed in real-world environments. These findings suggest that residual action modeling provides a simple yet powerful principle for bridging algorithmic advances in RL with the practical requirements of robotics.

</details>


### [93] [Robust Co-design Optimisation for Agile Fixed-Wing UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11130)
*Adrian Andrei Buda,Xavier Chen,Nicolò Botteghi,Urban Fasel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于敏捷固定翼无人机的鲁棒协同设计框架，将参数不确定性和风扰动直接集成到并发优化过程中，通过双层方法优化物理设计并评估性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同设计框架往往忽视自主系统在非结构化真实环境中所需的鲁棒性，导致设计对扰动和模型失配敏感，特别是对于在飞行包线边缘运行的敏捷无人机

Method: 采用双层方法：高层循环优化物理设计，通过约束轨迹规划器发现标称解，使用反馈LQR控制通过随机蒙特卡洛集成评估性能，直接集成参数不确定性和风扰动

Result: 在三个敏捷飞行任务中验证，该策略始终优于确定性基线，能够自动调整翼面位置和展弦比等气动特征，实现任务性能与扰动抑制之间的最优权衡

Conclusion: 鲁棒协同设计框架能够为敏捷固定翼无人机生成对不确定性和扰动具有鲁棒性的设计，通过同时优化物理设计和控制策略，实现性能与鲁棒性的平衡

Abstract: Co-design optimisation of autonomous systems has emerged as a powerful alternative to sequential approaches by jointly optimising physical design and control strategies. However, existing frameworks often neglect the robustness required for autonomous systems navigating unstructured, real-world environments. For agile Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) operating at the edge of the flight envelope, this lack of robustness yields designs that are sensitive to perturbations and model mismatch. To address this, we propose a robust co-design framework for agile fixed-wing UAVs that integrates parametric uncertainty and wind disturbances directly into the concurrent optimisation process. Our bi-level approach optimises physical design in a high-level loop while discovering nominal solutions via a constrained trajectory planner and evaluating performance across a stochastic Monte Carlo ensemble using feedback LQR control. Validated across three agile flight missions, our strategy consistently outperforms deterministic baselines. The results demonstrate that our robust co-design strategy inherently tailors aerodynamic features, such as wing placement and aspect ratio, to achieve an optimal trade-off between mission performance and disturbance rejection.

</details>


### [94] [ADMM-based Continuous Trajectory Optimization in Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11335)
*Lukas Pries,Jon Arrizabalaga,Zachary Manchester,Markus Ryll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于ADMM的数值求解器，用于非凸环境中连续轨迹规划，通过多项式参数化和时空分配图实现联合优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在非凸环境中轨迹规划存在局限性：解耦方法搜索空间有限，需要复杂预热初始化，收敛不可靠

Method: 定制化ADMM实现：1) 多项式参数化轨迹，原始更新可闭式求解为最小控制力问题；2) 基于混合整数规划的时空分配图，松弛更新转化为最短路径搜索

Result: 方法具有优于现有技术的优势：联合优化离散空间和连续时间域，访问更大搜索空间；结构鲁棒性确保从朴素初始化可靠收敛

Conclusion: 该求解器通过创新的多项式参数化和时空分配图方法，解决了非凸环境中轨迹规划的搜索空间限制和初始化瓶颈问题

Abstract: This paper presents a numerical solver for computing continuous trajectories in non-convex environments. Our approach relies on a customized implementation of the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) built upon two key components: First, we parameterize trajectories as polynomials, allowing the primal update to be computed in closed form as a minimum-control-effort problem. Second, we introduce the concept of a spatio-temporal allocation graph based on a mixed-integer formulation and pose the slack update as a shortest-path search. The combination of these ingredients results in a solver with several distinct advantages over the state of the art. By jointly optimizing over both discrete spatial and continuous temporal domains, our method accesses a larger search space than existing decoupled approaches, enabling the discovery of superior trajectories. Additionally, the solver's structural robustness ensures reliable convergence from naive initializations, removing the bottleneck of complex warm starting in non-convex environments.

</details>


### [95] [Novelty Adaptation Through Hybrid Large Language Model (LLM)-Symbolic Planning and LLM-guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11351)
*Hong Lu,Pierrick Lorang,Timothy R. Duggan,Jivko Sinapov,Matthias Scheutz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合符号规划、强化学习和大语言模型的神经符号架构，用于处理动态开放世界中机器人遇到新颖物体时的规划问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态开放世界环境中，自主智能体常遇到新颖物体，传统符号规划器因缺乏相应操作符而无法生成有效计划，需要一种能处理新颖物体的方法

Method: 提出神经符号架构，集成符号规划、强化学习和LLM：利用LLM的常识推理识别缺失操作符，符号AI规划器生成计划，LLM编写奖励函数指导RL智能体学习新操作符的控制策略

Result: 在连续机器人领域中，该方法在操作符发现和操作符学习方面优于现有最先进方法

Conclusion: 通过整合符号规划、强化学习和LLM的神经符号架构，能够有效处理动态开放世界中的新颖物体问题，提升机器人在未知环境中的适应能力

Abstract: In dynamic open-world environments, autonomous agents often encounter novelties that hinder their ability to find plans to achieve their goals. Specifically, traditional symbolic planners fail to generate plans when the robot's planning domain lacks the operators that enable it to interact appropriately with novel objects in the environment. We propose a neuro-symbolic architecture that integrates symbolic planning, reinforcement learning, and a large language model (LLM) to learn how to handle novel objects. In particular, we leverage the common sense reasoning capability of the LLM to identify missing operators, generate plans with the symbolic AI planner, and write reward functions to guide the reinforcement learning agent in learning control policies for newly identified operators. Our method outperforms the state-of-the-art methods in operator discovery as well as operator learning in continuous robotic domains.

</details>


### [96] [MirrorDrift: Actuated Mirror-Based Attacks on LiDAR SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11364)
*Rokuto Nagata,Kenji Koide,Kazuma Ikeda,Ozora Sako,Shion Horie,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MirrorDrift：首个针对生产级安全LiDAR的SLAM攻击，利用平面镜的镜面反射产生幽灵点云，系统性地偏置扫描匹配对应关系，无需信号注入即可破坏LiDAR SLAM定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR SLAM攻击依赖信号注入，需要传感器特定时序知识且易被现代防御机制（时序混淆、注入拒绝）缓解。本文探索无需信号注入的替代方案，利用镜面反射物理特性攻击LiDAR SLAM系统。

Method: 提出MirrorDrift攻击方法：使用可驱动的平面镜产生LiDAR扫描中的幽灵点，系统性地偏置扫描匹配对应关系。优化镜子放置、对准和驱动参数，在模拟中通过优化使平均位姿误差增加6.1倍。

Result: 模拟实验中，攻击使三种SLAM系统的平均位姿误差达到2.29-3.31米；真实世界实验中，对具有最先进干扰缓解机制的现代LiDAR诱导高达6.03米的定位误差。这是首个成功针对生产级安全LiDAR的SLAM攻击。

Conclusion: 镜面反射提供了一种无需信号注入的LiDAR SLAM攻击途径，能够有效绕过现代防御机制，对生产级安全LiDAR构成实际威胁，揭示了LiDAR SLAM系统在物理攻击下的新脆弱性。

Abstract: LiDAR SLAM provides high-accuracy localization but is fragile to point-cloud corruption because scan matching assumes geometric consistency. Prior physical attacks on LiDAR SLAM largely rely on LiDAR spoofing via external signal injection, which requires sensor-specific timing knowledge and is increasingly mitigated by modern defense mechanisms such as timing obfuscation and injection rejection. In this work, we show that specular reflection offers an injection-free alternative and demonstrate an attack, MirrorDrift, that uses an actuated planar mirror to cause ghost points in LiDAR scans and systematically bias scan-matching correspondences. MirrorDrift optimizes mirror placement, alignment, and actuation. In simulation, it increases the average pose error (APE) by 6.1x over random placement, degrading three SLAM systems to 2.29-3.31 m mean APE. In real-world experiments on a modern LiDAR with state-of-the-art interference mitigation, it induces localization errors of up to 6.03 m. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first successful SLAM-targeted attack against production-grade secure LiDARs.

</details>


### [97] [D-SLAMSpoof: An Environment-Agnostic LiDAR Spoofing Attack using Dynamic Point Cloud Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11365)
*Rokuto Nagata,Kenji Koide,Kazuma Ikeda,Ozora Sako,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出D-SLAMSpoof攻击方法，通过时空协调的激光欺骗干扰LiDAR SLAM系统，并在特征丰富的环境中提高攻击成功率；同时提出ISD-SLAM防御方法，仅使用惯性航位推算信号检测和缓解此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR欺骗攻击在特征丰富的环境（如城市和室内）中成功率低，而LiDAR SLAM系统在自动驾驶等关键应用中存在安全漏洞，需要研究更有效的攻击方法和实用防御方案。

Method: D-SLAMSpoof攻击：设计空间注入形状和基于扫描匹配原理的时间协调动态注入模式；ISD-SLAM防御：仅利用自动驾驶系统中常见的惯性航位推算信号进行检测和缓解。

Result: D-SLAMSpoof在真实世界特征丰富环境中显著提高攻击成功率；ISD-SLAM能够准确检测包括D-SLAMSpoof在内的LiDAR欺骗攻击，并有效缓解由此产生的位置漂移。

Conclusion: 揭示了LiDAR SLAM的内在脆弱性，提出了首个仅使用标准车载传感器的实用防御方案，为提升自动驾驶系统的安全性和可靠性提供了关键见解。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce Dynamic SLAMSpoof (D-SLAMSpoof), a novel attack that compromises LiDAR SLAM even in feature-rich environments. The attack leverages LiDAR spoofing, which injects spurious measurements into LiDAR scans through external laser interference. By designing both spatial injection shapes and temporally coordinated dynamic injection patterns guided by scan-matching principles, D-SLAMSpoof significantly improves attack success rates in real-world, feature-rich environments such as urban areas and indoor spaces, where conventional LiDAR spoofing methods often fail. Furthermore, we propose a practical defense method, ISD-SLAM, that relies solely on inertial dead reckoning signals commonly available in autonomous systems. We demonstrate that ISD-SLAM accurately detects LiDAR spoofing attacks, including D-SLAMSpoof, and effectively mitigates the resulting position drift. Our findings expose inherent vulnerabilities in LiDAR-based SLAM and introduce the first practical defense against LiDAR-based SLAM spoofing using only standard onboard sensors, providing critical insights for improving the security and reliability of autonomous systems.

</details>


### [98] [Vision-Based Hand Shadowing for Robotic Manipulation via Inverse Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11383)
*Hendrik Chiche,Antoine Jamme,Trevor Rigoberto Martinez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于单目RGB-D相机的离线手影重定向管道，用于低成本机械臂遥操作，通过手部关键点检测、3D重建、逆运动学求解实现机器人控制，在结构化任务中成功率90%，但在非结构化环境中因遮挡降至9.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本机械臂遥操作面临的主要挑战是如何将复杂的人手关节运动映射到机器人关节指令。现有方法需要复杂设备或训练数据，本文旨在开发一种基于单目RGB-D相机的无标记分析重定向管道。

Method: 使用安装在3D打印眼镜上的单目RGB-D相机采集手部数据；通过MediaPipe Hands检测每只手21个关键点；利用深度感知将2D关键点反投影到3D空间；将3D关键点转换到机器人坐标系；在PyBullet中使用阻尼最小二乘逆运动学求解生成6自由度SO-ARM101机器人的关节指令；设计四层回退机制的夹爪控制器，将拇指-食指几何映射到抓取开度；在物理仿真中预览动作后通过LeRobot框架在真实机器人上回放。

Result: 在结构化拾放基准测试（5格网格，每格10次抓取）中，逆运动学重定向管道达到90%成功率；与四种基于视觉-语言-动作策略（ACT、SmolVLA、pi0.5、GR00T N1.5）相比，这些策略使用领导者-跟随者遥操作数据训练；在非结构化真实环境（杂货店、药店）测试中，因周围物体遮挡手部，成功率降至9.3%（N=75）。

Conclusion: 无标记分析重定向管道在结构化任务中表现优异，但在非结构化环境中因手部遮挡而效果受限，显示了该方法的潜力和当前局限性，为未来改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Teleoperation of low-cost robotic manipulators remains challenging due to the complexity of mapping human hand articulations to robot joint commands. We present an offline hand-shadowing and retargeting pipeline from a single egocentric RGB-D camera mounted on 3D-printed glasses. The pipeline detects 21 hand landmarks per hand using MediaPipe Hands, deprojects them into 3D via depth sensing, transforms them into the robot coordinate frame, and solves a damped-least-squares inverse kinematics problem in PyBullet to produce joint commands for the 6-DOF SO-ARM101 robot. A gripper controller maps thumb-index finger geometry to grasp aperture with a four-level fallback hierarchy. Actions are first previewed in a physics simulation before replay on the physical robot through the LeRobot framework. We evaluate the IK retargeting pipeline on a structured pick-and-place benchmark (5-tile grid, 10 grasps per tile) achieving a 90% success rate, and compare it against four vision-language-action policies (ACT, SmolVLA, pi0.5, GR00T N1.5) trained on leader-follower teleoperation data. We also test the IK pipeline in unstructured real-world environments (grocery store, pharmacy), where hand occlusion by surrounding objects reduces success to 9.3% (N=75), highlighting both the promise and current limitations of marker-free analytical retargeting.

</details>


### [99] [Deployment-Time Reliability of Learned Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11400)
*Christopher Agia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该博士论文研究如何通过部署时机制提升学习型机器人策略的可靠性，包括运行时监控、数据驱动的可解释性框架和长时程任务执行协调


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管学习型机器人策略能力显著提升，但部署时的可靠性仍是实际应用的主要障碍。分布偏移、误差累积和复杂任务依赖关系共同影响系统性能，需要部署时机制来提升可靠性

Method: 提出三类部署时机制：1) 运行时监控方法，通过检测闭环策略行为不一致性和任务进度偏差来预测失败；2) 基于影响函数的数据中心化可解释性框架，将部署时成功/失败追溯到训练演示；3) 将策略协调形式化为估计和最大化行为序列成功概率的问题，并扩展到语言指定任务的可行性感知任务规划

Result: 这些机制共同提升了学习型机器人策略在部署时的可靠性，为实际应用提供了实用基础。具体结果包括：失败预测能力、策略行为诊断工具、长时程任务执行协调框架

Conclusion: 通过专注于部署核心挑战，这些贡献为学习型机器人策略的可靠实际应用推进了实用基础。这些基础的持续进展对于实现未来可信赖和可扩展的机器人自主性至关重要

Abstract: Recent advances in learning-based robot manipulation have produced policies with remarkable capabilities. Yet, reliability at deployment remains a fundamental barrier to real-world use, where distribution shift, compounding errors, and complex task dependencies collectively undermine system performance. This dissertation investigates how the reliability of learned robot policies can be improved at deployment time through mechanisms that operate around them. We develop three complementary classes of deployment-time mechanisms. First, we introduce runtime monitoring methods that detect impending failures by identifying inconsistencies in closed-loop policy behavior and deviations in task progress, without requiring failure data or task-specific supervision. Second, we propose a data-centric framework for policy interpretability that traces deployment-time successes and failures to influential training demonstrations using influence functions, enabling principled diagnosis and dataset curation. Third, we address reliable long-horizon task execution by formulating policy coordination as the problem of estimating and maximizing the success probability of behavior sequences, and we extend this formulation to open-ended, language-specified tasks through feasibility-aware task planning. By centering on core challenges of deployment, these contributions advance practical foundations for the reliable, real-world use of learned robot policies. Continued progress on these foundations will be essential for enabling trustworthy and scalable robot autonomy in the future.

</details>


### [100] [Real-time Rendering-based Surgical Instrument Tracking via Evolutionary Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11404)
*Hanyang Hu,Zekai Liang,Florian Richter,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于CMA-ES进化优化策略的手术器械跟踪框架，通过批量渲染并行评估多个位姿候选，实现高效准确的手术器械位姿和关节配置联合估计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助微创手术中，手术器械的准确高效跟踪至关重要。现有视觉方法在视觉质量下降和数据稀缺时特征检测不可靠，而渲染方法存在计算成本高和收敛性差的问题

Method: 将CMA-ES进化优化策略整合到跟踪流程中，通过批量渲染并行评估多个位姿候选，联合估计手术器械位姿和关节配置，支持无关节角度和双手操作跟踪设置

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在准确性和运行时间上显著优于现有方法，适用于视觉反馈控制和在线手术视频校准

Conclusion: 提出的基于CMA-ES的跟踪框架为手术器械跟踪提供了高效准确的解决方案，能够处理部分遮挡和特殊关节设计带来的挑战，具有实际应用价值

Abstract: Accurate and efficient tracking of surgical instruments is fundamental for Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Surgery. Although vision-based robot pose estimation has enabled markerless calibration without tedious physical setups, reliable tool tracking for surgical robots still remains challenging due to partial visibility and specialized articulation design of surgical instruments. Previous works in the field are usually prone to unreliable feature detections under degraded visual quality and data scarcity, whereas rendering-based methods often struggle with computational costs and suboptimal convergence. In this work, we incorporate CMA-ES, an evolutionary optimization strategy, into a versatile tracking pipeline that jointly estimates surgical instrument pose and joint configurations. Using batch rendering to efficiently evaluate multiple pose candidates in parallel, the method significantly reduces inference time and improves convergence robustness. The proposed framework further generalizes to joint angle-free and bi-manual tracking settings, making it suitable for both vision feedback control and online surgery video calibration. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms prior approaches in both accuracy and runtime.

</details>


### [101] [Grounding Robot Generalization in Training Data via Retrieval-Augmented VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11426)
*Jensen Gao,Dorsa Sadigh,Sandy Huang,Dhruv Shah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RADAR是一个可扩展框架，用于通过比较测试任务与训练数据来精确评估机器人策略的泛化能力，包含检索和VLM分析两阶段流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人操作研究中，策略泛化到新场景的评估往往难以准确表征测试设置与训练分布的实际差异，需要更精确的泛化评估方法。

Method: RADAR采用两阶段流程：1) 使用通用策略嵌入进行检索，识别与评估任务相关的训练示例；2) 视觉语言模型分析评估任务与检索数据的差异，输出可解释分析并分类所需泛化类型。

Result: 实验表明：1) VLM能有效分析泛化数据；2) 检索步骤能准确识别分类所需的训练示例；3) 在大规模数据集上，RADAR与人工定义的基准条件一致。

Conclusion: RADAR提供了一个可扩展框架，能够精确评估机器人策略泛化，通过系统比较测试任务与训练数据来确定所需的泛化类型，为机器人泛化评估提供了更精确的方法。

Abstract: Recent work on robot manipulation has advanced policy generalization to novel scenarios. However, it is often difficult to characterize how different evaluation settings actually represent generalization from the training distribution of a given policy. To work towards more precise evaluation of generalization in robotics, we propose RADAR, a scalable framework for directly comparing test-time evaluation tasks to policy training data, to determine what form of policy generalization is required. RADAR consists of a two-stage pipeline: first, retrieval using generalist policy embeddings identifies which training examples are relevant for a given evaluation task. Next, vision-language models (VLMs) analyze the evaluation task against the retrieved data, outputting interpretable analysis on how they compare along a variety of axes, and an overall classification of what type of policy generalization is required. Through controlled experiments, we demonstrate that VLMs are effective at analyzing data for generalization, and that our retrieval step effectively identifies examples needed to make accurate classifications with respect to the training data. Furthermore, we scale RADAR to large-scale datasets, where we observe agreement with human-defined benchmark conditions from prior work. We provide demonstrations at radar-analysis.github.io.

</details>


### [102] [A Generalized Theory of Load Distribution in Redundantly-actuated Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11431)
*Joshua Flight,Clément Gosselin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一个广义理论，描述冗余驱动机器人系统中刚性体的载荷分布，解决了多指抓取器、多足机器人等过约束机构的力控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在描述冗余驱动机器人系统中刚性体的载荷分布时存在不足，特别是对于多指抓取器、多足机器人、协作机器人等过约束机构，需要更完善的理论框架来表征可行的操纵力分布。

Method: 提出了一个广义理论，完全表征了施加到刚性体上的给定合力对应的可行操纵力分布集。推导了力合成和力分析问题的显式解，这些解计算效率高，与施加力数量呈线性关系，无需数值方法或大矩阵求逆。

Result: 理论能够完全描述刚性体在冗余驱动系统中的载荷分布，显式解具有线性计算复杂度。识别了当前最先进方法的重大缺陷并提出了修正，通过示例证明了改进方法的优势。

Conclusion: 该理论为冗余驱动机器人系统的力控制提供了完整框架，显式解具有高效计算特性，修正了现有方法的缺陷，对多指抓取器、多足机器人等过约束机构的力控制有重要应用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a generalized theory which describes how applied loads are distributed within rigid bodies handled by redundantly-actuated robotic systems composed of multiple independent closed-loop kinematic chains. The theory fully characterizes the feasible set of manipulating wrench distributions for a given resultant wrench applied to the rigid body and has important implications for the force-control of multifingered grippers, legged robots, cooperating robots, and other overconstrained mechanisms. We also derive explicit solutions to the wrench synthesis and wrench analysis problems. These solutions are computationally efficient and scale linearly with the number of applied wrenches, requiring neither numerical methods nor the inversion of large matrices. Finally, we identify significant shortcomings in current state-of-the-art approaches and propose corrections. These are supported by illustrative examples that demonstrate the advantages of the improved methods.

</details>


### [103] [Enhancing Lightweight Vision Language Models through Group Competitive Learning for Socially Compliant Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11447)
*Xinyu Zhang,Atsushi Konno,Toshihiko Yamasaki,Ling Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Group Competitive Learning (GCL)策略，通过Group Competitive Objective (GCO)和Asymmetric Group Optimization (AGO)增强轻量级VLM在社交导航中的推理能力，实现高性能与计算效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人导航需要结合场景语义和人类社交规范。大型视觉语言模型(VLMs)推理能力强但计算开销大，不适合实时部署；轻量级VLMs效率高但推理能力弱。如何在保持高效率的同时获得强推理能力是一个开放挑战。

Method: 提出Group Competitive Learning (GCL)策略，包含两个核心组件：1) Group Competitive Objective (GCO)：协调全局语义与分布正则化；2) Asymmetric Group Optimization (AGO)：探索模型性能上限。该方法旨在增强轻量级VLMs的能力。

Result: 在社交导航基准测试中，GCL显著提升了VLM性能：Qwen2.5-VL-3B模型F1分数达到0.968（比SFT提升40%），Qwen3-VL-4B达到0.914（提升12%）。值得注意的是，经过GCL训练的3B模型超越了8B基线模型（提升28%），证明了该方法在保持高效率的同时实现高性能的能力。

Conclusion: GCL为在现实世界部署中同时实现高精度和计算效率提供了有效解决方案，解决了轻量级VLMs在复杂社交环境中推理能力不足的问题，使实时社交机器人导航成为可能。

Abstract: Social robot navigation requires a sophisticated integration of scene semantics and human social norms. Scaling up Vision Language Models (VLMs) generally improves reasoning and decision-making capabilities for socially compliant navigation. However, increased model size incurs substantial computational overhead, limiting suitability for real-time robotic deployment. Conversely, lightweight VLMs enable efficient inference but often exhibit weaker reasoning and decision-making performance in socially complex environments. Achieving both strong reasoning ability and efficiency remains an open challenge. To bridge this gap, we propose Group Competitive Learning (GCL), a strategy designed to amplify the capabilities of lightweight VLMs. Our strategy introduces the Group Competitive Objective (GCO) to harmonize global semantics with distributional regularization, alongside Asymmetric Group Optimization (AGO) to explore the upper limits of model performance. Empirical evaluations on social navigation benchmarks demonstrate that GCL significantly elevates VLM performance. Specifically, GCL enables the Qwen2.5-VL-3B learner model and guide Qwen3-VL-4B to achieve an F1 score of 0.968 and 0.914, representing 40\% and 12\% improvement over vanilla supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Notably, under vanilla SFT, the 3B model initially trails the 8B model (F1: 0.692 vs. 0.755). However, through the GCL, the 3B model outperforms (28\%) the 8B baseline model. These results suggest that GCL provides an effective solution for achieving both high accuracy and computational efficiency in real-world deployment.

</details>


### [104] [NFPO: Stabilized Policy Optimization of Normalizing Flow for Robotic Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11470)
*Diyuan Shi,Yiqi Tang,Zifeng Zhuang,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NFPO：使用归一化流作为策略参数化的深度强化学习方法，解决了传统高斯策略无法建模多模态分布的问题，在机器人学习任务中表现出色并能迁移到真实机器人


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度强化学习中广泛使用的多元高斯策略（对角协方差矩阵）缺乏建模多模态分布的能力，限制了策略的表达能力

Method: 采用归一化流作为策略参数化方法，利用其多模态建模能力、对数概率闭式解以及低计算内存开销的优势，并通过简单有效的技术解决了在线强化学习中训练不稳定的问题

Result: 在多个仿真环境中进行广泛实验，NFPO方法在广泛使用的机器人学习任务中获得了鲁棒且强大的性能，并成功迁移到真实机器人

Conclusion: 归一化流作为策略参数化是一种有效的深度强化学习方法，能够建模多模态分布，解决传统高斯策略的局限性，在机器人学习任务中具有实际应用价值

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has experienced significant advancements in recent years and has been widely used in many fields. In DRL-based robotic policy learning, however, current de facto policy parameterization is still multivariate Gaussian (with diagonal covariance matrix), which lacks the ability to model multi-modal distribution. In this work, we explore the adoption of a modern network architecture, i.e. Normalizing Flow (NF) as the policy parameterization for its ability of multi-modal modeling, closed form of log probability and low computation and memory overhead. However, naively training NF in online Reinforcement Learning (RL) usually leads to training instability. We provide a detailed analysis for this phenomenon and successfully address it via simple but effective technique. With extensive experiments in multiple simulation environments, we show our method, NFPO could obtain robust and strong performance in widely used robotic learning tasks and successfully transfer into real-world robots.

</details>


### [105] [SPARK: Skeleton-Parameter Aligned Retargeting on Humanoid Robots with Kinodynamic Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11480)
*Hanwen Wang,Qiayuan Liao,Bike Zhang,Kunzhao Ren,Koushil Sreenath,Xiaobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段流程：通过骨架校准和动力学轨迹优化，从人类运动数据生成自然且动力学可行的机器人运动参考


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类运动数据为训练人形机器人控制策略提供丰富先验，但原始演示与机器人运动学和动力学不兼容，限制了直接使用

Method: 两阶段流程：1) 将人类运动转换为URDF骨架表示并校准到目标人形机器人尺寸；2) 通过渐进式动力学轨迹优化（分为运动学TO、逆动力学和完整动力学TO三个阶段）精炼重定向轨迹

Result: 显著减少逆运动学误差和调参工作量，生成动态一致的状态轨迹和关节扭矩剖面，为基于学习的控制器提供高质量参考

Conclusion: 骨架校准和动力学轨迹优化相结合，能够为不同人形平台生成自然、物理一致的运动参考

Abstract: Human motion provides rich priors for training general-purpose humanoid control policies, but raw demonstrations are often incompatible with a robot's kinematics and dynamics, limiting their direct use. We present a two-stage pipeline for generating natural and dynamically feasible motion references from task-space human data. First, we convert human motion into a unified robot description format (URDF)-based skeleton representation and calibrate it to the target humanoid's dimensions. By aligning the underlying skeleton structure rather than heuristically modifying task-space targets, this step significantly reduces inverse kinematics error and tuning effort. Second, we refine the retargeted trajectories through progressive kinodynamic trajectory optimization (TO), solved in three stages: kinematic TO, inverse dynamics, and full kinodynamic TO, each warm-started from the previous solution. The final result yields dynamically consistent state trajectories and joint torque profiles, providing high-quality references for learning-based controllers. Together, skeleton calibration and kinodynamic TO enable the generation of natural, physically consistent motion references across diverse humanoid platforms.

</details>


### [106] [MiNI-Q: A Miniature, Wire-Free Quadruped with Unbounded, Independently Actuated Leg Joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11537)
*Daniel Koh,Suraj Shah,Yufeng Wu,Dennis Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MiNI-Q²是一款微型无线四足机器人，具有独立驱动的机械无限制2自由度腿部关节，能够实现多种运动模式并折叠至2.5厘米高度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腿式机器人的物理关节限制会限制工作空间、约束步态设计并增加硬件损坏风险，因此需要开发具有无限制关节的机器人来克服这些限制。

Method: 提出MiNI-Q²机器人的机械设计、运动学分析和实验验证，采用独立驱动的机械无限制2自由度腿部关节，无线架构改进了之前的Q8bot设计，提高了微型尺度的装配可靠性。

Result: MiNI-Q²最高速度达到0.46 m/s，能够实现低间隙爬行、爬楼梯、倒置行走、跳跃和后空翻等多种运动能力，最小折叠高度为2.5厘米。

Conclusion: MiNI-Q²展示了无限制关节在微型四足机器人中的优势，实现了多样化的运动能力，所有机械和电气设计文件开源发布以支持可重复性和进一步研究。

Abstract: Physical joint limits are common in legged robots and can restrict workspace, constrain gait design, and increase the risk of hardware damage. This paper introduces MiNI-Q^2, a miniature, wire-free quadruped robot with independently actuated, mechanically unbounded 2-DOF leg joints. We present the mechanical design, kinematic analysis, and experimental validation of the proposed robot. The leg mechanism enables both oscillatory gaits and rotary locomotion while allowing the robot to fold to a minimum height of 2.5 cm. Experimentally, MiNI-Q achieves speeds up to 0.46 m/s and demonstrates low-clearance crawling, stair climbing, inverted locomotion, jumping, and backflipping. The wire-free architecture extends our previous Q8bot design, improving assembly reliability at miniature scale. All mechanical and electrical design files are released open source to support reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [107] [RoboClaw: An Agentic Framework for Scalable Long-Horizon Robotic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11558)
*Ruiying Li,Yunlang Zhou,YuYao Zhu,Kylin Chen,Jingyuan Wang,Sukai Wang,Kongtao Hu,Minhui Yu,Bowen Jiang,Zhan Su,Jiayao Ma,Xin He,Yongjian Shen,Yangyang,Guanghui Ren,Maoqing Yao,Wenhao Wang,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboClaw是一个统一的VLA机器人框架，通过纠缠动作对实现自主数据收集和策略学习，在长时程任务中显著提升成功率和减少人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA系统在扩展到长时程任务时面临挑战，传统流水线将数据收集、策略学习和部署分离，导致严重依赖人工环境重置和多策略执行的脆弱性。

Method: 提出RoboClaw框架，在单一VLM驱动控制器下统一数据收集、策略学习和任务执行。引入纠缠动作对(EAP)，将前向操作行为与逆向恢复动作耦合，形成自主重置循环，实现连续在线数据收集和迭代策略优化。

Result: 在真实世界操作任务中，相比传统开环流水线，RoboClaw提高了稳定性和可扩展性，长时程任务成功率提升25%，人工时间投入减少53.7%。

Conclusion: RoboClaw通过统一的VLM驱动控制器和纠缠动作对机制，解决了VLA系统在长时程任务中的扩展问题，显著减少了机器人生命周期中的人工干预，提高了多策略执行的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems have shown strong potential for language-driven robotic manipulation. However, scaling them to long-horizon tasks remains challenging. Existing pipelines typically separate data collection, policy learning, and deployment, resulting in heavy reliance on manual environment resets and brittle multi-policy execution. We present RoboClaw, an agentic robotics framework that unifies data collection, policy learning, and task execution under a single VLM-driven controller. At the policy level, RoboClaw introduces Entangled Action Pairs (EAP), which couple forward manipulation behaviors with inverse recovery actions to form self-resetting loops for autonomous data collection. This mechanism enables continuous on-policy data acquisition and iterative policy refinement with minimal human intervention. During deployment, the same agent performs high-level reasoning and dynamically orchestrates learned policy primitives to accomplish long-horizon tasks. By maintaining consistent contextual semantics across collection and execution, RoboClaw reduces mismatch between the two phases and improves multi-policy robustness. Experiments in real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improved stability and scalability compared to conventional open-loop pipelines, while significantly reducing human effort throughout the robot lifecycle, achieving a 25% improvement in success rate over baseline methods on long-horizon tasks and reducing human time investment by 53.7%.

</details>


### [108] [Unsupervised LiDAR-Based Multi-UAV Detection and Tracking Under Extreme Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11586)
*Nivand Khosravi,Rodrigo Ventura,Meysam Basiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无监督的LiDAR检测与跟踪流程，专门针对固态LiDAR扫描稀疏（每扫描仅1-2个点）的空中无人机检测场景，无需标注数据，通过真实飞行数据验证检测性能，并通过仿真评估多目标跟踪方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态LiDAR的非重复扫描导致对空中无人机的测量极其稀疏（10-25米距离的小型四旋翼通常每扫描仅产生1-2个回波点），远低于现有检测方法假设的点云密度，且不足以进行鲁棒的多目标数据关联。

Method: 1. 检测器：集成距离自适应DBSCAN聚类与三阶段时间一致性检查；2. 多目标跟踪：比较确定性匈牙利分配与联合概率数据关联（JPDA），均与交互多模型滤波结合；3. 评估策略：结合真实世界检测（RTK-GPS地面真值）与基于仿真的跟踪（身份标注地面真值）。

Result: 最佳检测配置达到0.891精度、0.804召回率和0.63米RMSE；minPts扫描验证大多数扫描最多包含1-2个目标点；JPDA在四种仿真场景中将身份切换减少64%，对MOTA影响可忽略，证明当无人机轨迹接近时概率关联具有优势。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无监督LiDAR检测与跟踪流程，专门解决固态LiDAR稀疏测量下的空中无人机检测问题；通过真实飞行数据验证了检测性能，并通过仿真表明概率数据关联在轨迹接近时优于确定性方法；双环境评估策略克服了GNSS评估在2米以下间距的局限性。

Abstract: Non-repetitive solid-state LiDAR scanning leads to an extremely sparse measurement regime for detecting airborne UAVs: a small quadrotor at 10-25 m typically produces only 1-2 returns per scan, which is far below the point densities assumed by most existing detection approaches and inadequate for robust multi-target data association. We introduce an unsupervised, LiDAR-only pipeline that addresses both detection and tracking without the need for labeled training data. The detector integrates range-adaptive DBSCAN clustering with a three-stage temporal consistency check and is benchmarked on real-world air-to-air flight data under eight different parameter configurations. The best setup attains 0.891 precision, 0.804 recall, and 0.63 m RMSE, and a systematic minPts sweep verifies that most scans contain at most 1-2 target points, directly quantifying the sparsity regime. For multi-target tracking, we compare deterministic Hungarian assignment with joint probabilistic data association (JPDA), each coupled with Interacting Multiple Model filtering, in four simulated scenarios with increasing levels of ambiguity. JPDA cuts identity switches by 64% with negligible impact on MOTA, demonstrating that probabilistic association is advantageous when UAV trajectories approach one another closely. A two-environment evaluation strategy, combining real-world detection with RTK-GPS ground truth and simulation-based tracking with identity-annotated ground truth, overcomes the limitations of GNSS-only evaluation at inter-UAV distances below 2 m.

</details>


### [109] [Diversity You Can Actually Measure: A Fast, Model-Free Diversity Metric for Robotics Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11634)
*Sreevardhan Sirigiri,Nathan Samuel de Lara,Christopher Agia,Florian Shkurti,Fabio Ramos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FAKTUAL算法，通过基于签名核的熵度量来量化机器人模仿学习数据集的多样性，并利用该度量进行高效的数据筛选，提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习数据集通常包含不同长度的长时程轨迹和高维观测（如RGB视频），难以在尊重轨迹结构和几何特性的前提下量化多样性。现有方法缺乏直接操作演示数据集的熵和多样性度量。

Method: 扩展香农熵和冯·诺依曼熵，基于签名核的Gram矩阵定义签名变换熵，提出FAKTUAL算法：通过最大化熵来选择给定预算下的演示子集。该方法完全无模型，无需访问模仿策略或rollout。

Result: 在图像和状态基础的RoboMimic、MetaWorld基准以及四个真实世界操作任务上评估。FAKTUAL的多样性感知筛选相比随机选择持续提升下游成功率，且计算效率显著高于现有机器人数据筛选方法。

Conclusion: 演示数据集的熵是理解和改进机器人模仿学习中数据集多样性的实用工具。FAKTUAL提供了一种简单、无模型、高效的数据筛选方法，能显著提升模仿学习性能。

Abstract: Robotics datasets for imitation learning typically consist of long-horizon trajectories of different lengths over states, actions, and high-dimensional observations (e.g., RGB video), making it non-trivial to quantify diversity in a way that respects the underlying trajectory structure and geometry. We extend Shannon and von Neumann entropy to this setting by defining signature transform-based entropy on the Gram matrix of a signature kernel over demonstrations, yielding entropy and diversity metrics that operate directly on the demonstration dataset. Building on these metrics, we study how dataset diversity affects generalization performance in robot imitation learning and propose a simple, model-free way to curate diverse demonstrations. We introduce FAKTUAL (FAst trajectory Kernel enTropy cUration for imitation Learning), a data curation algorithm that selects a subset of demonstrations maximizing entropy given a subset-size budget. FAKTUAL is fully model-free, requires no access to the imitation policy or rollouts, and adds negligible overhead relative to policy training. We evaluate our approach on image and state-based RoboMimic and MetaWorld benchmarks, as well as four real-world manipulation tasks. Across tasks and architectures, diversity-aware curation with FAKTUAL consistently improves downstream success rates over random selection, while being substantially more computationally efficient compared to recent robot data curation methods. Our results suggest that the entropy of demonstration datasets is a practical tool for understanding and improving dataset diversity in robot imitation learning.

</details>


### [110] [Learn Structure, Adapt on the Fly: Multi-Scale Residual Learning and Online Adaptation for Aerial Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11638)
*Samaksh Ujjawal,Naveen Sudheer Nair,Shivansh Pratap Singh,Rishabh Dev Yadav,Wei Pan,Spandan Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Factorized Dynamics Transformer (FDT)和Latent Residual Adapter (LRA)的预测-自适应框架，用于自主空中机械臂的非线性、非平稳动力学实时建模与补偿


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空中机械臂具有固有的耦合非线性特性，在机械臂重构和突加载荷变化时表现出非平稳、多尺度的残余动力学。传统解析模型依赖固定参数结构，静态数据驱动模型假设平稳动力学，在配置变化和载荷变化时性能下降。现有学习架构未能显式分解跨变量耦合和多尺度时间效应，混淆了瞬时惯性动力学与长期状态演化。

Method: 提出预测-自适应框架，核心是Factorized Dynamics Transformer (FDT)，将物理变量作为独立token处理，显式实现跨变量注意力，结构上分离短时程惯性依赖与长时程气动效应。为应对部署时的分布偏移，采用Latent Residual Adapter (LRA)通过递归最小二乘法在潜空间进行快速线性自适应，保留离线非线性表示而不引入过高计算开销。自适应残余预测直接集成到残余补偿自适应控制器中。

Result: 在承受未知载荷的空中机械臂上进行真实世界实验，相比最先进的学习基线方法，展示了更高的预测保真度、更快的扰动衰减速度、更优的闭环跟踪精度，同时保持严格的实时可行性。

Conclusion: 提出的FDT-LRA框架有效解决了自主空中机械臂的非线性、非平稳动力学建模问题，通过显式分解跨变量耦合和多尺度时间效应，结合快速线性自适应机制，实现了在真实世界条件下的高性能实时控制。

Abstract: Autonomous Aerial Manipulators (AAMs) are inherently coupled, nonlinear systems that exhibit nonstationary and multiscale residual dynamics, particularly during manipulator reconfiguration and abrupt payload variations.
